check('X1a 湖水量与湖盆沉积都抬高湖水位（ℓ = WL/AL + kV·V）；湖水位同时改变 P、Q 两条水路的渠段回水坡（读）与冲淤写入（写），并使湖口潜在出流为正、大水拍兑现出流为正',  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;all(rows\[k\]\[0\] \> rows\['ι'\]\[0\] for k in KS)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;and all(rows\[k\]\[i\] != rows\['ι'\]\[i\] for k in KS for i in (1, 2, 3, 4)) and all(rows\[k\]\[5\] \> 0 and rows\[k\]\[6\] \> 0 for k in KS) and rows\['ι'\]\[5\] == 0)  
wdry = hf.step(wv, 'R')  
check('X1c 反例 F21-5（出口边界）：WL = 0、V = 32 的静置拍，潜在出流 %s 而可供水量为 0，兑现出流为 0，末水量 %s；出口下切按兑现出流写入（c\_D 不变：%s）'  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;% (fs(hf.outflow\_pot(wv)), fs(wdry\['WL'\]), fs(wdry\['cD'\])), hf.outflow(wv, 'R') == 0 and wdry\['WL'\] == 0 and wdry\['cD'\] == wv\['cD'\])  
hc = X.GeoX(par={'clamp': True})  
check('X1b 钳定臂在同一改动下湖水位、两路冲淤与出流都不变（钳定只切断湖盆状态的读出，记账照常）',

~~~python
      all(hc.terms(w, 'B')[1][j]['dc'] == hc.terms(wi, 'B')[1][j]['dc'] for j in 'PQ' for w in (wl, wv)) and all(hc.outflow_pot(w) == hc.outflow_pot(wi) for w in (wl, wv)))

# ============================================================ X2
def traj(par, forcing=FORCING, w=None, periodic=False, metric='out'):
    """沿固定入流走一条轨迹并登记。periodic：每个事件块（事件拍 + 3 静置拍）之后清除 P 路口坝（m_P := 0，移出物外运，计入 removed）；
    metric：职责判据的沙通量（'out' 出渠，'basin' 实际入湖）。另记沙账（P 路出渠、入湖盆合计、外运、移除）与 N 首次实际过半流量的事件。"""
    h = X.GeoX(par=par)
    w = dict(w0 if w is None else w)
    hold0 = X.role_holder(h, w, TH, metric)
    reg = dict(s='', who=[], kind=[], avul=None, mother=None, beat=None, states=[], maj=None, majev=None,
               acc=dict(QQ=Fr(0), dcQ=Fr(0), QD=Fr(0), QP=Fr(0), QN=Fr(0), outP=Fr(0), basin=Fr(0), export=Fr(0), removed=Fr(0)))
    beat = 0
    for b in forcing:
        if b != 'F':
            if reg['maj'] is None and h.exists(w, 'N') and X.majority(h, w, b):
                reg['maj'], reg['majev'] = len(reg['s']) + 1, b
            hold = X.role_holder(h, w, TH, metric)
            kinds = X.same_kind(h, w)
            c = X.classify(hold0, hold, kinds)
            reg['s'] += CODE[c]
            reg['who'].append(''.join(hold))
            reg['kind'].append(''.join(kinds))
            reg['states'].append(w)
            m = h.avulsion_due(w, b)
            if m is not None and reg['avul'] is None:
                reg['avul'], reg['mother'], reg['beat'] = len(reg['s']) - 1, m, beat
        for e in H([b]):
            y = h.act(w, e)
            if e in h.ev:
                _s, t, _q = h.terms(w, e)
                reg['acc']['QQ'] += y['QQ']
                reg['acc']['dcQ'] += t['Q']['dc']
                reg['acc']['QP'] += y['QP']
                reg['acc']['QN'] += y['QN']
                reg['acc']['outP'] += t['P']['out']
                reg['acc']['basin'] += sum(t[j]['basin'] for j in t)
                reg['acc']['export'] += sum(t[j]['export'] for j in t)
            reg['acc']['QD'] += y['QD']
            w = h.step(w, e)
            beat += 1
        if periodic and b != 'F':
            reg['acc']['removed'] += w['mP']
            w = dict(w, mP=Fr(0))
    reg['end'] = w
    reg['hold0'] = hold0
    reg['host'] = h
    return reg

print('\nX2　固定入流（%d 年，每年 冻融·大水·小水·小水），湖盆反馈开启臂与钳定臂（ℓ ≡ 0）对照；读另一水路 Q 与湖口出流渠 D' % YEARS)
ON = traj({})
CL = traj({'clamp': True})
for nm, r in (('反馈开启', ON), ('钳定', CL)):
    e = r['end']
    print('  %s：初始职责承担者 %s；逐事件登记 %s' % (nm, r['hold0'], r['s']))
    print('    Q 路事件通流累计 %s，事件冲淤累计 %s；P 路通流累计 %s；N 路通流累计 %s；湖口出流累计 %s；末态 c_Q %s、c_D %s、湖水位 %s'
          % (fl(r['acc']['QQ']), fl(r['acc']['dcQ']), fl(r['acc']['QP']), fl(r['acc']['QN']), fl(r['acc']['QD']), fl(e['cQ']), fl(e['cD']), fl(r['host'].level(e))))
check('X2a 两臂入流相同而另一水路读数不同：反馈开启臂 Q 路的事件通流累计大于钳定臂（Q 的渠段冲淤两臂都为 0，未过冲刷阈，如实分报）；湖口出流渠 D 只在开启臂出流并下切（钳定臂 ℓ₀ 低于溢流槛）',
      ON['acc']['QQ'] > CL['acc']['QQ'] and ON['acc']['dcQ'] == CL['acc']['dcQ'] == 0 and ON['acc']['QD'] > 0 and ON['end']['cD'] > 0
      and CL['acc']['QD'] == 0 and CL['end']['cD'] == 0)
check('X2b 钳定臂：全程不决口、原路径 P 保持职责（逐事件全为「原路径保持」）', CL['avul'] is None and set(CL['s']) == {'O'})
# ============================================================ X3
print('\nX3　沿实际轨迹登记形成、改道、接续与失效（反馈开启臂；职责与判据事前冻结，见正文 11.4）')
k_av = ON['avul']
wa = ON['states'][k_av]
print('  改道：第 %d 次事件（全史第 %d 拍）为大水，母路 %s；拍前 x_%s − c_%s = %s ≥ Hs·(h0 + x_%s) = %s；湖水位 %s'
      % (k_av + 1, ON['beat'], ON['mother'], ON['mother'], ON['mother'], fl(wa['x' + ON['mother']] - wa['c' + ON['mother']]), ON['mother'],
         fl(X.XBASE['Hs'] * (X.XBASE['h0'] + wa['x' + ON['mother']])), fl(ON['host'].level(wa))))
s = ON['s']
first_N = s.index('N')
print('  四类分报（事件数）：原路径保持 %d，新路径承担同一职责 %d，只有同类关系 %d，职责中断 %d'
      % tuple(s.count(c) for c in 'ONkx'))
print('  过渡期（改道后至新路首次承担）：' + ' '.join('%s[%s]' % (s[i], ON['kind'][i] or '—') for i in range(k_av + 1, first_N)))
print('  新路首次承担：第 %d 次事件；此后至窗末 %d 次事件全为新路承担 %s' % (first_N + 1, len(s) - first_N, set(s[first_N:]) == {'N'}))
EVSEQ = [b for b in FORCING if b != 'F']
print('  「改道」三个时点分登记：新路开通 第 %d 次事件（%s）；新路首次在实际来水中取得过半流量 第 %d 次事件（%s）；新路首次满足完整职责 第 %d 次事件（%s）'
      % (k_av + 1, EVSEQ[k_av], ON['maj'], ON['majev'], first_N + 1, EVSEQ[first_N]))
check('X3f 开通、首次过半、首次完整职责三时点不同：开通在大水（第 13 次），首次过半在其后的小水（第 14 次），首次完整职责在第 26 次——不得以开通发生于大水推称过半也在大水',
      (k_av + 1, ON['maj'], first_N + 1) == (13, 14, 26) and EVSEQ[k_av] == 'B' and ON['majev'] == 'S')
shN, soN = [], []
for i in range(k_av + 1, first_N):
    for e in ('B', 'S'):
        _sg, tt, qq = ON['host'].terms(ON['states'][i], e)
        shN.append(qq['N'] / ON['host'].ev[e][0])
        soN.append(tt['N']['out'] / sum(tt[j]['out'] for j in tt))
print('  过渡期新路 N 的份额（两类来水）：通流 %s—%s；出渠沙不低于 %s' % (fl(min(shN)), fl(max(shN)), fl(min(soN))))
check('X3e 过渡期所缺的是通流份额：N 的出渠沙份额全程 ≥ θ，而通流份额全程 < θ（输沙职责先于通流职责转移）',
      min(soN) >= TH and max(shN) < TH)
check('X3a 改道为内生事件：发生在反馈开启臂的大水拍，母路为原承担者 P；改道前 P 一直保持职责',
      k_av is not None and ON['mother'] == 'P' and set(s[:k_av + 1]) == {'O'})
check('X3b 四类在同一轨迹上都出现且分报：原路径保持 → 改道后过渡期只有同类关系与职责中断交替 → 新路径 N 承担同一职责并保持到窗末',
      all(c in s for c in 'ONkx') and set(s[k_av + 1:first_N]) <= {'k', 'x'} and set(s[first_N:]) == {'N'}
      and all(w == 'N' for w in ON['who'][first_N:]))
we = ON['end']
_s, tE, qE = ON['host'].terms(we, 'B')
print('  废弃路愈合：改道时 c_P %s、u_P %s → 窗末 c_P %s、u_P %s；窗末大水 P 分流 %s / %s'
      % (fl(wa['cP']), fl(wa['uP']), fl(we['cP']), fl(we['uP']), fl(qE['P']), fs(ON['host'].ev['B'][0])))
check('X3c 废弃路愈合：窗末 P 的沟口下切浅于改道时，渠段的河道内淤积（c_P < 0）向平原面回复（|c_P| 减小），P 在大水中的分流份额低于 θ（失效登记）',
      we['uP'] < wa['uP'] and wa['cP'] < 0 and abs(we['cP']) < abs(wa['cP']) and qE['P'] < TH * ON['host'].ev['B'][0])
NA = traj({'avulse': False})
print('  禁止决口臂（反馈开启）：%s' % NA['s'])
check('X3d 机制的必要性读数（所测轨迹）：反馈开启而禁止决口时，无新路承担；P 的职责在后期间歇丧失（出现「只有同类关系」），不出现新路径承担',
      'N' not in NA['s'] and 'k' in NA['s'] and NA['s'][:k_av + 1] == s[:k_av + 1])
# ============================================================ X4
print('\nX4　条件表：湖口出流能力 ko 与溢流槛 z0（其余同缺省；入流相同）')
table = {}
for ko, z0 in itertools.product((Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)), (Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2))):
    r = traj({'ko': ko, 'z0': z0})
    tail = r['s'][-10:]
    table[(ko, z0)] = (r['avul'], tail, r['host'].level(r['end']))
    print('  ko = %s，z0 = %s：改道 %s；末 10 次事件 %s；末湖水位 %s' % (fs(ko), fs(z0), '第 %d 次事件' % (r['avul'] + 1) if r['avul'] is not None else '无', tail, fl(table[(ko, z0)][2])))
av = lambda ko, z0: table[(ko, z0)][0]
~~~

check('X4a 条件性：出流能力 ko ≥ 1 时两种槛高都不决口、末段原路径保持；ko ≤ 1/2 时除 (1/2, 1/4) 外都决口且末段新路径承担',

~~~text
      all(av(k, z) is None and set(table[(k, z)][1]) == {'O'} for k in (Fr(1), Fr(2)) for z in (Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)))
      and av(Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 4)) is None
      and all(av(k, z) is not None and set(table[(k, z)][1]) == {'N'} for (k, z) in ((Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 4)), (Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)), (Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)))))
check('X4b 所测网格上的单调读数：同一 ko 下槛高越高改道越早（或由无到有）；同一槛高下出流能力越低改道越早（或由无到有）',
      av(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)) < av(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 4)) and av(Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 4)) is None and av(Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)) is not None
      and av(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 4)) is not None and av(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)) <= av(Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)))
check('X4c 末湖水位与结局同向：凡末段新路径承担者，其末湖水位都高于一切末段原路径保持者',
      min(v[2] for v in table.values() if set(v[1]) == {'N'}) > max(v[2] for v in table.values() if set(v[1]) == {'O'}))
# ============================================================ X5
print('\nX5　三类沙去向在湖盆反馈开启时的对照：同起点 w₀、同入流、同参数、同决口开关；四臂 × 反馈开启／钳定成对')
ARMS = [('无干预', {}, False), ('周期移除口坝', {}, True), ('持续禁止口坝沉积、改配入湖（β_P = 0）', {'betaP': Fr(0)}, False),
        ('持续禁止口坝沉积、外运（β_P = 0，外运）', {'betaP': Fr(0), 'exportP': True}, False)]
arm = {}
for fb, cl in (('反馈开启', False), ('钳定', True)):
    for nm, par, per in ARMS:
        r = traj(dict(par, clamp=cl), periodic=per)
        rb = traj(dict(par, clamp=cl), periodic=per, metric='basin')
        fN = r['s'].index('N') + 1 if 'N' in r['s'] else None
        arm[(fb, nm)] = (r, rb, fN)
        a = r['acc']
        print('  %s｜%s：改道 %s；N 首次过半 %s；N 首次承担同一职责 %s；登记串（出渠判据）%s；入湖判据登记串相同 %s'
              % (fb, nm, '第 %d 次事件' % (r['avul'] + 1) if r['avul'] is not None else '无', '第 %d 次' % r['maj'] if r['maj'] else '—',
                 '第 %d 次' % fN if fN else '—', r['s'], rb['s'] == r['s']))
        print('    沙账：P 路出渠 %s；入湖盆合计 %s；外运 %s；周期移除 %s；末 V %s；末湖位 %s'
              % (fl(a['outP']), fl(a['basin']), fl(a['export']), fl(a['removed']), fl(r['end']['V']), fl(r['host'].level(r['end']))))
cl_all = all(arm[('钳定', nm)][0]['avul'] is None and set(arm[('钳定', nm)][0]['s']) == {'O'} for nm, _p, _r in ARMS)
check('X5a 钳定臂：四臂都不决口，45 次事件全为原路径保持（湖位不反馈时，三类处理与无干预在所测协议的指定读数——改道与逐事件职责登记——上不可区分；物质账不同）', cl_all)
on = {nm: arm[('反馈开启', nm)] for nm, _p, _r in ARMS}
av_on = {nm: (on[nm][0]['avul'] + 1 if on[nm][0]['avul'] is not None else None) for nm in on}
fn_on = {nm: on[nm][2] for nm in on}
print('  反馈开启：改道事件 %s；N 首次承担 %s' % (av_on, fn_on))
check('X5b 反馈开启：无干预第 13 次事件改道，三类处理都推迟到第 16 次；N 首次承担同一职责：无干预第 26 次，改配入湖第 27 次，外运与周期移除第 28 次',
      av_on == {'无干预': 13, '周期移除口坝': 16, '持续禁止口坝沉积、改配入湖（β_P = 0）': 16, '持续禁止口坝沉积、外运（β_P = 0，外运）': 16}
      and fn_on == {'无干预': 26, '周期移除口坝': 28, '持续禁止口坝沉积、改配入湖（β_P = 0）': 27, '持续禁止口坝沉积、外运（β_P = 0，外运）': 28})
lv = {nm: on[nm][0]['host'].level(on[nm][0]['end']) for nm in on}
~~~

check('X5c 「三处理等效」在反馈开启时被打破：同一观察末点（第 15 年末）三类处理的末湖位两两不同（改配入湖 %s 最高，外运 %s，周期移除 %s），首次承担时点不全相同'

~~~text
      % (fl(lv['持续禁止口坝沉积、改配入湖（β_P = 0）']), fl(lv['持续禁止口坝沉积、外运（β_P = 0，外运）']), fl(lv['周期移除口坝'])),
      len({lv[nm] for nm, _p, _r in ARMS[1:]}) == 3 and lv['持续禁止口坝沉积、改配入湖（β_P = 0）'] > max(lv['周期移除口坝'], lv['持续禁止口坝沉积、外运（β_P = 0，外运）'])
      and len({fn_on[nm] for nm, _p, _r in ARMS[1:]}) > 1)
check('X5d 两种沙通量判据（出渠、实际入湖）在八臂上的职责登记串逐位相同；物质账不同（改配入湖的入湖盆合计大于外运臂，外运臂外运 > 0）',
      all(arm[k][1]['s'] == arm[k][0]['s'] for k in arm)
      and on['持续禁止口坝沉积、改配入湖（β_P = 0）'][0]['acc']['basin'] > on['持续禁止口坝沉积、外运（β_P = 0，外运）'][0]['acc']['basin']
      and on['持续禁止口坝沉积、外运（β_P = 0，外运）'][0]['acc']['export'] > 0)
print('  声明：本节只报形成史与职责登记；未在 𝔐21⁺ 上冻结扰动挑战，不对未来受扰动支配（Dom）作结论。')
# ============================================================ 汇总
print('\n' + '=' * 60)
print('核验 %d 项：通过 %d，失败 %d' % (len(RES), sum(RES), len(RES) - sum(RES)))
sys.exit(0 if all(RES) else 1)
~~~

A.6　dm21\_run\_law.py　运行入口三：成律回读（节 L0—L8）

~~~python
"""《地貌具化场律生成数理式》v1.0 附录程序：一条候选关系的成律回读（模型侧）运行入口（局部模型 𝔐21；dm21_law.py）。
运行：python3 dm21_run_law.py（与 dm21_run.py、dm21_run_lake.py 分入口）
节：L0 冻结申报；L1 同一判据查形成前后（首次取得的时点）；L2 Established 逐项；L3 DynamicRole 的目标与增量对照；
    L4 ΩSupport 的段级三项事实与按类别标记的读法 Ω^c；L5 SC_Hold 的途径二（申报当前能力量）在申报历史族上的穷举核验；
    L5d 无外部维护的双入口循环（另一种当前能力）；L6 干预：同一 R 的结构正端首次、退出或复得，水路关系、复走窗口与当前能力分栏；
    L7 失效条件的推导读数；L8 预测层的直接物理读数（两类来水的主要通流份额、完整职责、结构正端分读）。签署范围：冻结模型 𝔐21、正文 8.5 的申报与工作域、所列时点。
每项输出「通过／失败」；末尾汇总；任一项失败时退出码为 1。只用标准库；一切数值为 Fraction；无随机。"""
import os
import sys
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
~~~

sys.path\[:0\] = \[HERE\]

~~~python
import jhc_core as JH
import dm21_model as G
import dm21_law as L
RES = []

def check(label, ok):
    RES.append(bool(ok))
    print('%s　%s' % ('通过' if ok else '失败', label))

fs = JH.fstr

def fl(x):
    return '%.4f' % float(x)

g = G.Geo()
gc = G.Geo(par={'closed': ('P',)})
TH = L.TH
DIST = [('id', None), ('bar', {'mP': Fr(1, 16)}), ('bank', {'cP': -Fr(1, 16)}), ('fill', {'uP': -Fr(1, 16)})]
HLEN = 10          # SC_Hold 途径二的申报历史族：自初始地势起、长度 ≤ HLEN 的一切 {B, S} 事件序列（每事件后 3 个静置拍）
print('《地貌具化场律生成数理式》v1.0 附录程序运行输出：成律回读（模型侧，局部模型 𝔐21）')
# ============================================================ L0
print('\nL0　冻结申报（计算前写定，正文 8.5）')
print('  候选律 L*：Rel(L*) = R*_θ(P)，θ = 2/3：事件 i 到来时 P 路承担主要通流（Q_P ≥ θQ_i）且主要出渠输沙（S_P > 0 且 S_P ≥ θ(S_P + S_Q)）')
print('  适用覆盖域 I = I_cov = {B, S}；承载组织 R = {u_P, c_P}；R 外迹应位 {m_P, u_Q, c_Q, m_Q}；窗 W = 8 宏步（以 t 为末端），等分 2 段，复走记录判据 ≥ 2')
print('  合法反事实族（DynamicRole）：回退相对静置参照的增量（保留预置基底；双位、单 u_P、单 c_P）与设回基态；他位固定')
print('  工作域：𝔐21 缺省参数；h 为供沙边界，于一切反事实与设回中保持实际值不变；V 只记账不回写')
print('  当前能力量（SC_Hold 途径二）：Cap_8 = Dom_8(P8)：输入条件 𝓒_2；维持条件 𝒟₄、预算 1；评价期限 N = 8 宏步；判据 N + 1 次读 R* 全成立')
w0 = dict(g.init)
~~~

check('L0a 起初未成立：初始地势上 R\*\_θ(P) 对两类来水都不成立（大水份额 %s，小水份额 %s）' % (fl(g.share(w0, 'B')), fl(g.share(w0, 'S'))),

~~~text
      not L.read(g, w0, 'B') and not L.read(g, w0, 'S'))
# ============================================================ L1
print('\nL1　同一判据查形成前后：逐宏步末的 Established、DynamicRole、ΩSupport（正式三项事实）与三者合取')
HIST = [('H1', 'B' * 8 + 'S' * 8), ('H1m', 'B' * 8 + 'SB' * 4), ('H★', 'B' * 8 + 'SB' * 8), ('H2', 'S' * 8 + 'B' * 8)]
RD = {}
for nm, seq in HIST:
    rows, ms, rs = L.law_readout(g, seq)
    RD[nm] = (rows, ms, rs, seq)
    print('  %s：Established %s；DynamicRole %s；ΩSupport %s；合取 %s；回退增量翻转读出 %s；首次合取 %s'
          % (nm, ''.join(str(r['Est']) for r in rows), ''.join(str(r['DR']) for r in rows), ''.join(str(r['Om']) for r in rows),
             ''.join(str(r['Est'] & r['DR'] & r['Om']) for r in rows), ''.join(str(int(r['flip'])) for r in rows), L.first_all(rows)))
r1 = RD['H1'][0]
t1 = L.first_all(r1)
~~~

check('L1a H1 中三者合取首次为 1 于第 %s 宏步末，此后至窗末保持；此前每一宏步末至少一项为 0（第 0—3 宏步 Established 为 0，第 4—9 宏步 ΩSupport 为 0）' % t1,

~~~text
      t1 == 10 and all(r['Est'] & r['DR'] & r['Om'] for r in r1[10:])
      and all(r['Est'] == 0 for r in r1[:4]) and all(r['Om'] == 0 for r in r1[4:10]))
print('  前段事件序列：H1 第 9、10 次事件为 %s，H1m、H★ 为 %s（三史第 10 宏步末同一判定，但前段不同）' % (RD['H1'][3][8:10], RD['H1m'][3][8:10]))
check('L1b H1m 与 H★ 的首次合取同为第 10 宏步末（与 H1 判定相同而第 9、10 次事件不同：H1 为 SS，H1m、H★ 为 SB）；H2 在一切宏步末合取为 0（Established 恒为 0）',
      L.first_all(RD['H1m'][0]) == 10 and L.first_all(RD['H★'][0]) == 10 and all(r['Est'] == 0 for r in RD['H2'][0])
      and RD['H1'][3][8:10] == 'SS' and RD['H1m'][3][8:10] == 'SB' and RD['H★'][3][8:10] == 'SB')
rH, msH, rsH, seqH = RD['H1']
print('  H1 第 9、10 宏步末的 ΩSupport 三项事实：%s，记录 %s；%s，记录 %s'
      % (rH[9]['od'], rH[9]['orec']['recs'], rH[10]['od'], rH[10]['orec']['recs']))
check('L1c 第 9 宏步末缺的是第三项事实（窗 [1, 9) 的前半 [1, 5) 只有第一次承担通过 4，无复走记录）；第 10 宏步末三项事实俱成立',
      rH[9]['od']['f1'] and rH[9]['od']['f2'] and not rH[9]['od']['f3'] and all(rH[10]['od'].values()))
# ============================================================ L2
print('\nL2　Established 逐项（H1 第 10 宏步末）')
e10 = rH[10]['ed']
print('  CurrentCarrier %s；CarrierRealizes（每一 i：读出成立、R 设回基态即不成立、R 外迹应位设回基态仍成立）%s；CarrierNonInstant %s；沉积承载（〔十七〕三分）%s'
      % (e10['CC'], e10['CR'], e10['CNI'], e10['SED']))
check('L2a Established 四项在 H1 第 10 宏步末逐项成立：R 两位深度越 ε；对 B、S 各自，读出成立、R 设回基态即不成立、R 外迹应位设回基态仍成立；宏步末无即时驱动；两位三分为迹应载体',
      rH[10]['Est'] == 1 and all(all(v) for v in e10['CR'].values()) and all(v == '迹应载体' for v in e10['SED'].values()))
first_est = next(t for t, r in enumerate(rH) if r['Est'])
print('  H1 第 %d 宏步末 Established 首次为 1；第 3 宏步末：%s' % (first_est, rH[3]['ed']['CR']))
check('L2b Established 首次为 1 于第 4 宏步末（与 Dom_8 的首次时点相同）；第 3 宏步末不成立的是 CarrierRealizes 的「读出成立」一项',
      first_est == 4 and not all(rH[3]['ed']['CR'][i][0] for i in L.I_))
# ============================================================ L3
print('\nL3　DynamicRole：目标为该职责的实际活动（Q_P、S_P）与写回（Δu_P、Δc_P）；增量对照保留预置基底')
dw10 = rH[10]['dw']
for i, nm, ch, flip in dw10:
    print('  第 10 宏步末，来水 %s，%s：活动与写回发生改变（Q_P, S_P, Δu_P, Δc_P）= %s；读出翻转 %s' % (i, nm, ch, flip))
check('L3a H1 第 10 宏步末：两类来水下，「回退 u_P、c_P 相对参照的增量（保留预置基底）」使 Q_P、S_P、Δu_P、Δc_P 全部改变且读出翻转——承担职责的是历史写入的增量，不是预置旧槽',
      all(all(ch) and flip for i, nm, ch, flip in dw10 if nm == '回退增量（u_P、c_P）'))
~~~

~~~python
def dfs(seq, ms):

    for c in 'BS':
        dfs(seq + [c], ms + [G.macro(g, ms[-1], c, 3)])

def cycle(w, period, cap=256):
    """自 w 起按 period 交替（每事件后 3 静置拍），以（相位、一切非 V 位）为精确键找回环；每步先读后走。
    返回 ('回环', 首见步, 再见步) 或 ('失败', 已续行步)。"""
    seen = {}
    v = w
    test = 'BS' if len(period) > 1 else 'S'
    for k in range(cap):
        key = (k % len(period), tuple(v[x] for x in g.X if x != 'V'))
        if key in seen:
            return ('回环', seen[key], k)
        if not all(L.read(g, v, e) for e in test):
            return ('失败', k)
        seen[key] = k
        v = G.macro(g, v, period[k % len(period)], 3)
    return ('未定', cap)

CYC = {}
for nm, t_ in (('H1', 10), ('H1m', 15)):
    for per in ('BS', 'SB', 'S'):
        CYC[(nm, per)] = cycle(RD[nm][1][t_], per)
        print('  %s 第 %d 宏步末起，序列 %s：%s' % (nm, t_, per + '…' if len(per) > 1 else '只来小水', CYC[(nm, per)]))

SEQS = 'B' * 8 + 'SB' * 8
print('  历史 H★ =（B·R³）⁸（S·R³·B·R³）⁸；结构正端首次于第 10 宏步末。迹应干预施于第 k 宏步末（第 k 宏步的静置拍之后、第 k + 1 次事件之前）；')
print('  断路 @ k：程序中 t == k 的事件拍（事件从 1 编号即第 k + 1 次事件）P 的通导置 0，自第 k 宏步末之后开始、至该事件拍结束；第 k 宏步末的读数已计入该边界的零通导')

def run_int(k, kind, parts=False):
    w = dict(g.init)
    ref = dict(w)
    ms, rs, flw, cnd = [w], [ref], [], []
    for t, e in enumerate(SEQS):
        h = gc if (kind == '断路' and t == k) else g
        flw.append(h.act(w, e)['QP'])
        cnd.append(h.cond(w, 'P'))
        w = G.macro(h, w, e, 3)
        for _ in range(4):
            ref = g.step(ref, 'R')
        if t + 1 == k:
            if kind == '回退双位增量':
                w = dict(w, uP=ref['uP'], cP=ref['cP'])
            elif kind == '回退 u_P 增量':
                w = dict(w, uP=ref['uP'])
            elif kind == '回退 c_P 增量':
                w = dict(w, cP=ref['cP'])
            elif kind == '抹去口坝':
                w = dict(w, mP=Fr(0))
        ms.append(w)
        rs.append(ref)
    cnd.append(g.cond(w, 'P'))
    rows, _m, _r = L.law_readout(g, SEQS, ms=ms, rs=rs, flows=flw, conds=cnd)
    if parts == 'rows':
        return rows, ms
    if parts:
        return ''.join(str(r['Est']) for r in rows), ''.join(str(r['Om']) for r in rows)
    return ''.join(str(r['Est'] & r['DR'] & r['Om']) for r in rows)

base = run_int(-1, '无')
print('  无干预：结构正端 %s' % base)
KINDS = ('回退双位增量', '回退 u_P 增量', '回退 c_P 增量', '抹去口坝', '断路')
KS = (2, 4, 6, 8, 12, 16)
TAB, PARTS, CAPV = {}, {}, {}
CAPN = dict(bad=0)
for kind in KINDS:
    for k in KS:
        rows_k, ms_k = run_int(k, kind, parts='rows')
        s_ = ''.join(str(r['Est'] & r['DR'] & r['Om']) for r in rows_k)
        TAB[(kind, k)] = s_
        PARTS[(kind, k)] = (''.join(str(r['Est']) for r in rows_k), ''.join(str(r['Om']) for r in rows_k))
        for t, (r, w) in enumerate(zip(rows_k, ms_k)):
            if t < k or not (r['Est'] & r['DR'] & r['Om']):
                continue
            key = tuple(w[x] for x in g.X if x != 'V')
            if key not in CAPV:
                CAPV[key] = G.dom_N(g, w, TH, 2, 3, DIST, 8, 1)[0]
            CAPN['bad'] += CAPV[key] != '1'
        f_ = s_.find('1')
        if k < 10:
            note = '结构正端首次 %s' % ('第 %d 宏步' % f_ if f_ >= 0 else '25 宏步内未出现')
        else:
            lost = [t for t in range(k, len(s_)) if s_[t] == '0']
            rec = next((t for t in range(lost[0], len(s_)) if s_[t] == '1'), None) if lost else None
            note = '结构正端：%s' % ('未退出' if not lost else '第 %d 宏步退出，%s' % (lost[0], '第 %d 宏步复得' % rec if rec else '25 宏步内未复得'))
        print('  %s @ 第 %d 宏步：结构正端 %s；水路关系（Established）%s；复走窗口（ΩSupport）%s（%s）' % (kind, k, s_, PARTS[(kind, k)][0], PARTS[(kind, k)][1], note))

def first(s_):
    return s_.find('1') if '1' in s_ else None

def lost_after(s_, k):
    return any(s_[t] == '0' for t in range(k, len(s_)))
~~~

~~~python
print('\nL8　预测层的直接物理读数（H★，0—24 宏步末共 25 个读点）：两类来水的主要通流（Q_P/Q ≥ θ，B、S 都达）、完整职责（Read_B ∧ Read_S）、结构正端分读')
PHY = {}
for kind in ('回退双位增量', '回退 u_P 增量', '回退 c_P 增量', '断路'):
    for k in ((2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 12, 16) if kind == '回退双位增量' else (12, 16)):
        rows_k, ms_k = run_int(k, kind, parts='rows')
        st_ = ''.join(str(r['Est'] & r['DR'] & r['Om']) for r in rows_k)
        fw_ = ''.join(str(int(all(g.share(w, e) >= TH for e in L.I_))) for w in ms_k)
        rl_ = ''.join(str(int(all(L.read(g, w, e) for e in L.I_))) for w in ms_k)
        PHY[(kind, k)] = (fw_, rl_, st_)
        print('  %s @ 第 %d 宏步末：两类主要通流 %s；完整职责 %s；结构正端 %s' % (kind, k, fw_, rl_, st_))
        if kind == '断路':
            nxt = SEQS[k + 1]
            PHY[('next', k)] = L.read(g, ms_k[k + 1], nxt)
            PHY[('cutflow', k)] = gc.act(ms_k[k], SEQS[k])['QP']
            print('    断路事件（第 %d 次事件）本身 P 的实际通流 %s' % (k + 1, fs(PHY[('cutflow', k)])))
            print('    撤闸后下一次来水（第 %d 次事件，%s）：完整职责的该类读数 %s' % (k + 2, nxt, PHY[('next', k)]))

def rec_at(s_, k):
    return next((t for t in range(k, len(s_)) if s_[t] == '1'), None)

f12, r12, s12 = PHY[('回退双位增量', 12)]
f16, r16, s16 = PHY[('回退双位增量', 16)]
~~~

check('L8a 第 12 宏步末双位回退：两类主要通流与完整职责在第 12—19 宏步末为 0，第 20 宏步末恢复并保持至第 24 宏步末；结构正端截至第 24 宏步末未复得',  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;f12\[12:20\] == '0' \* 8 and f12\[20:\] == '1' \* 5 and r12 == f12 and '1' not in s12\[12:\])  
check('L8b 第 16 宏步末双位回退：两类主要通流与完整职责在第 24 宏步末恢复；结构正端截至第 24 宏步末未复得',  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rec\_at(f16, 16) == 24 and r16 == f16 and '1' not in s16\[16:\])  
fc, rc, sc = PHY\[('回退 c\_P 增量', 12)\]  
check('L8c 单位回退可提前恢复：第 12 宏步末只回退 c\_P，两类主要通流第 13 宏步末恢复，结构正端第 14 宏步末复得（物理与结构时点分离）',

~~~text
      rec_at(fc, 13) == 13 and fc[12] == '0' and rec_at(sc, 13) == 14)
check('L8d 初次形成前的双位回退（第 2—8 宏步末全部七个读点：k = 2、3、4、5、6、7、8）：干预之后直到第 24 宏步末两类主要通流都未取得',
      all('1' not in PHY[('回退双位增量', k)][0][k:] for k in (2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8)))
check('L8e 断路（第 13、17 次事件）：实际断路事件本身 P 的通流为零；各宏步末以正常通导读的两类能力都成立；撤闸后的下一次来水（大水）完整职责读数成立；结构正端按窗口滞后（k = 12：第 21 宏步末复得）',
      PHY[('next', 12)] and PHY[('next', 16)] and PHY[('cutflow', 12)] == 0 and PHY[('cutflow', 16)] == 0 and PHY[('断路', 12)][0][4:] == '1' * 21 and PHY[('断路', 12)][2][21] == '1')
wb = dict(RD['H1'][1][10])
bnd = wb['mQ'] + (1 - TH) * g.ev['S'][0] / g.cond(wb, 'Q')
we_ = dict(wb, mP=JH.floor_grid(bnd, g.grid) + Fr(1, g.grid))
sh_ = {e: g.share(we_, e) for e in L.I_}
print('  P21-11 对照：H1 第 10 宏步末口坝抬至小水必要界上一格，大水份额 %s（职责 %s）、小水份额 %s（职责 %s）'
      % ('%.6f' % float(sh_['B']), L.read(g, we_, 'B'), '%.6f' % float(sh_['S']), L.read(g, we_, 'S')))
check('L8f P21-11 对照：大水份额约 0.700908 而职责通过，小水份额约 0.574806 而职责失败——「低流先失」限本对照（高流仍达阈值）；普遍可推出的是低流必要界',
      L.read(g, we_, 'B') and not L.read(g, we_, 'S') and abs(float(sh_['B']) - 0.700908) < 1e-6 and abs(float(sh_['S']) - 0.574806) < 1e-6)
# ============================================================ 汇总
print('\n' + '=' * 60)
~~~

A.7　jhc\_core.py　上游冻结源码：〔十七〕《具化场识别判定式》v1.0 附录 A.1

~~~python
"""《具化场识别判定式》v1.0 附录程序：核心库。
只用 Python 标准库；数值一律 fractions.Fraction。宿载模型的接口：X（位）、lo/hi（夹限）、ground（无迹基态）、
orient（申报取向）、init、grid（取格）、act(w, s)（活动）、comps(w, y, s)（分项列表 (过程, 位, 值)）。
  branches          两支分报：A 支（只换活动）、D 支（活动换后再改直读），A + D = 总差（按未钳定机制重算）。
  branches_rev      另一次序的望远镜分解（先改直读，再换活动）。
  hc_total          差异读法：存在合法设定使总分项差 > ε（不限可用位；只作对照）。
  avail_at          拍前态中已越阈的承担位（定义 6′(乙) 的数值部分）。
  hc_status         有效差异证书的三种判值：有效差异已获证／函数支持已立／有效差异未获证。
  hdyn_cert         同拍、同一过程上的 Act 与有效差异（定义 6′、定义 7′ 的模型模式）。
  sed               活动中介正分项（可选加强台账）；plus 为 SED⁺。
  net_deep          实际净加深（另一台账）。
  trace_candidates  迹应候选筛查（取向深度 > ε 与 < −ε 分列；不是元素证书）。
  retention         自留存：已观测下界与完整判词分开；完整须显式尾部证据（定义 2′）。
  triage            按下界、完整与否和申报 τ_act、τ_W 给三分判词；只有下界且不足 τ_W 时判 ⊥_Tail。
  org_graph_exact   在申报的全部联合设定上精确枚举（含超边）。
  org_graph_sample  有界采样：只报见证边与覆盖缺口。
  basins            有限链上映射的吸引域（按「极限等于该不动点」的原像）。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
from itertools import combinations
import math
ZERO = Fr(0)
FRACS = (Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(2))

def floor_grid(x, G):
    return Fr(math.floor(x * G), G)

class Host:
    name = 'host'
    grid = None
    def step(self, w, s):
        y = self.act(w, s)
        new = dict(w)
        for (_p, x, c) in self.comps(w, y, s):
            new[x] = new[x] + c
        for x in self.X:
            v = min(self.hi[x], max(self.lo[x], new[x]))
            if self.grid is not None:
                v = floor_grid(v, self.grid)
            new[x] = v
        return new

def run(host, w0, hist):
    out, w = [dict(w0)], dict(w0)
    for s in hist:
        w = host.step(w, s)
        out.append(w)
    return out

def ref_hist(host, hist):
    return ['rest' for _ in hist]

def depth(host, w, x):
    """取向深度（带号）：正值为申报取向上的迹应，负值为越过基态的另一型。"""
    return host.orient[x] * (w[x] - host.ground[x])

def trace_candidates(host, hist, eps):
    """候选筛查（不是元素证书）。返回 (轨迹, 参照轨迹, 正向候选, 反向候选, 信号形成位)。"""
    S = run(host, host.init, hist)
    R = run(host, host.init, ref_hist(host, hist))
    w, wr = S[-1], R[-1]
    pos = [x for x in host.X if depth(host, w, x) > eps]
    neg = [x for x in host.X if depth(host, w, x) < -eps]
    sig = [x for x in host.X if abs(w[x] - wr[x]) > eps]
    return S, R, pos, neg, sig

def retention(seq, eps, tail=None):
    """自留存（定义 2′）。返回 (下界, 判别)；下界 = 记录中最后一个 |u| > eps 的 t 加一（无越阈为 0）。
    判别为 '完整' 当且仅当给出并核过尾部证据，否则为 '下界'——记录末点越阈与否都一样：
    末点不越阈不排除此后静置中再越阈。tail 取以下之一：
      ('覆盖',)                记录已覆盖余下的全部静置域（申报，函数不代证）；
      ('不变集', step, band)   band 为阈下集的全部点（|u| ≤ eps），step 为静置一步映射；
                               须记录末点在 band 中且 step(band) ⊆ band（逐点精确核）；
      ('包络', C, lam)         已证 |u(t_end + s)| ≤ C·lam^s（s ≥ 0），0 ≤ lam < 1；须 C ≤ eps。"""
    last = 0
    for t, v in enumerate(seq):
        if abs(v) > eps:
            last = t + 1
    if tail is None or not seq:
        return last, '下界'
    kind = tail[0]
    ok = False
    if kind == '覆盖':
        ok = True
    elif kind == '不变集':
        step, band = tail[1], set(tail[2])
        ok = (seq[-1] in band and all(abs(u) <= eps for u in band)
              and all(step(u) in band for u in band))
    elif kind == '包络':
        C, lam = tail[1], tail[2]
        ok = 0 <= lam < 1 and C <= eps and abs(seq[-1]) <= C
    return last, ('完整' if ok else '下界')

def triage(lb, status, tau_act, tau_W):
    """可被写入的载体的三分判词（定义 2′）：下界 ≥ τ_W 即迹应载体；活动与活动残留须完整自留存；
    只有下界而下界 < τ_W 时判 ⊥_Tail（不签活动、残留或非迹应）。"""
    if lb >= tau_W:
        return '迹应载体'
    if status != '完整':
        return '⊥_Tail'
    return '活动' if lb <= tau_act else '活动残留'

def setv(host, w, vals):
    v = dict(w)
    for x, val in vals.items():
        v[x] = min(host.hi[x], max(host.lo[x], val))
    return v

def setT(host, w, T, frac=Fr(0)):
    return setv(host, w, {x: host.ground[x] + frac * (w[x] - host.ground[x]) for x in T})

def comp_of(host, w, y, s, proc, x):
    tot = ZERO
    for (p, xx, c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
        if p == proc and xx == x:
            tot += c
    return tot

def total_comp(host, w, s, proc, x):
    return comp_of(host, w, host.act(w, s), s, proc, x)

def branches(host, w, w2, s, proc, x):
    """两支分报（对照 w2 为合法设定后的状态）。A = c(w 直读, y(w2)) − c(w 直读, y(w))；
    D = c(w2 直读, y(w2)) − c(w 直读, y(w2))；A + D = 总差 = c(w2) − c(w)（按未钳定机制重算）。"""
    y, y2 = host.act(w, s), host.act(w2, s)
    c0 = comp_of(host, w, y, s, proc, x)
    cA = comp_of(host, w, y2, s, proc, x)
    c2 = comp_of(host, w2, y2, s, proc, x)
    return cA - c0, c2 - cA, c2 - c0

def settings(host, w, T, fracs=FRACS, joint=True):
    """申报的合法设定族：T 的每一非空子集（joint=True）或单位与全体（joint=False）乘以深度倍数。"""
    T = list(T)
    subs = []
    if joint:
        for k in range(1, len(T) + 1):
            subs += [list(c) for c in combinations(T, k)]
    else:
        subs = [[x] for x in T] + ([T] if len(T) > 1 else [])
    out = []
    for U in subs:
        for f in fracs:
            w2 = setT(host, w, U, f)
            if w2 != w:
                out.append((tuple(U), f, w2))
    return out

def hc_total(host, w, s, proc, x, T, eps, fracs=FRACS, joint=True):
    """历史条件的差异读法：存在合法设定使总分项差 > eps。返回 (U, f, A, D, 总差) 或 None。"""
    for (U, f, w2) in settings(host, w, T, fracs, joint):
        A, D, tot = branches(host, w, w2, s, proc, x)
        if abs(tot) > eps:
            return (U, f, A, D, tot)
    return None

def part(host, w, T, probes, eps, fracs=FRACS):
    for s in probes:
        y0 = host.act(w, s)
        for (U, f, w2) in settings(host, w, T, fracs, joint=False):
            y1 = host.act(w2, s)
            for j in y0:
                if abs(y0[j] - y1[j]) > eps:
                    return (s, j, y0[j], y1[j])
    return None

def hdyn(host, states, sigs, T, eps, fracs=FRACS):
    """对照用：窗内某拍过程 p 对 x ∈ T 的分项有效，且总分项差在 T 的某合法设定下有效（不限可用位）。"""
    for k, s in enumerate(sigs):
        w = states[k]
        y = host.act(w, s)
        seen = set()
        for (p, x, c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
            if x not in T or (p, x) in seen:
                continue
            seen.add((p, x))
            c = comp_of(host, w, y, s, p, x)
            if abs(c) <= eps:
                continue
            h = hc_total(host, w, s, p, x, T, eps, fracs)
            if h:
                return (k, s, p, x, c) + h
    return None

def sed(host, reach, T, eps, plus=False, fracs=FRACS):
    """活动中介正分项（可选加强台账）：(甲) 取向分项 > eps；(乙) 同一设定上总差有效且 A 支有效。
    plus：另要求把整个 T 按各 frac < 1 设浅都使取向分项严格变小（自增强台账；不是对一切子集的全称）。"""
    for (w, s) in reach:
        y = host.act(w, s)
        for (p, x, c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
            if x not in T:
                continue
            c = comp_of(host, w, y, s, p, x)
            if host.orient[x] * c <= eps:
                continue
            ok = False
            for (U, f, w2) in settings(host, w, T, fracs):
                A, D, tot = branches(host, w, w2, s, p, x)
                if abs(tot) > eps and abs(A) > eps:
                    ok = True
                    break
            if not ok:
                continue
            if not plus:
                return (s, p, x, c)
            good = True
            for f_ in [f_ for f_ in fracs if f_ < 1]:
                w2 = setT(host, w, T, f_)
                if not host.orient[x] * total_comp(host, w2, s, p, x) < host.orient[x] * c:
                    good = False
                    break
            if good:
                return (s, p, x, c)
    return None

def net_deep(host, w, protocol, T, eps):
    """实际净加深：施协议后 T 中某位取向深度之增 > eps。返回 [(x, 前, 后)]。"""
    w2 = dict(w)
    for s in protocol:
        w2 = host.step(w2, s)
    return [(x, depth(host, w, x), depth(host, w2, x)) for x in T if depth(host, w2, x) - depth(host, w, x) > eps]

def reach_pairs(host, hists):
    out = []
    for h in hists:
        S = run(host, host.init, h)
        for k, s in enumerate(h):
            out.append((S[k], s))
    return out

class UF:
    def __init__(self, items):
        self.p = {i: i for i in items}
    def f(self, i):
        while self.p[i] != i:
            self.p[i] = self.p[self.p[i]]
            i = self.p[i]
        return i
    def u(self, a, b):
        ra, rb = self.f(a), self.f(b)
        if ra != rb:
            self.p[rb] = ra

def _act_dep(host, w, w2, s, eps):
    y0, y1 = host.act(w, s), host.act(w2, s)
    return [j for j in y0 if abs(y0[j] - y1[j]) > eps]

def org_graph_exact(host, w, E, probes, eps, fracs=FRACS):
    """精确枚举：在 E 的全部非空子集 × 深度倍数（申报的联合设定族）上求依赖。
    共同参与超边：使活动分量 j 改变的极小子集 U（|U| ≥ 2 时为超边，|U| = 1 时并入同一 j 的单位依赖群）。
    历史条件超边：使作用于 x 的总分项改变的极小子集 U，连同 x。返回 (超边列表, 分支)。"""
    E = list(E)
    hyper = set()
    for s in probes:
        y0 = host.act(w, s)
        changed = {}
        for (U, f, w2) in settings(host, w, E, fracs, joint=True):
            for j in _act_dep(host, w, w2, s, eps):
                changed.setdefault(('act', j), set()).add(U)
            for (p, x, c) in host.comps(w, y0, s):
                if x not in E:
                    continue
                tot = total_comp(host, w2, s, p, x) - comp_of(host, w, y0, s, p, x)
                if abs(tot) > eps:
                    changed.setdefault(('w', p, x), set()).add(U)
        for key, Us in changed.items():
            mins = [U for U in Us if not any(set(V) < set(U) for V in Us)]
            if key[0] == 'act':
                singles = sorted({U[0] for U in mins if len(U) == 1})
                if len(singles) >= 2:
                    hyper.add(('共同参与', key[1], tuple(singles)))
                for U in mins:
                    if len(U) >= 2:
                        hyper.add(('共同参与', key[1], tuple(sorted(U))))
            else:
                x = key[2]
                for U in mins:
                    nodes = tuple(sorted(set(U) | {x}))
                    if len(nodes) >= 2:
                        hyper.add(('历史条件', key[1] + '@' + x, nodes))
    uf = UF(E)
    for (_k, _n, nodes) in hyper:
        for a in nodes[1:]:
            uf.u(nodes[0], a)
    comps = {}
    for i in E:
        comps.setdefault(uf.f(i), []).append(i)
    return sorted(hyper), sorted([sorted(v) for v in comps.values()])

def org_graph_sample(host, w, E, probes, eps, tested, fracs=FRACS):
    """有界采样：只在 tested（子集列表）上设定。返回 (见证超边, 覆盖缺口：未被任何已测子集共同覆盖的位对；被覆盖也不等于已识别)。"""
    hyper = set()
    for s in probes:
        y0 = host.act(w, s)
        for U in tested:
            for f in fracs:
                w2 = setT(host, w, U, f)
                if w2 == w:
                    continue
                for j in _act_dep(host, w, w2, s, eps):
                    hyper.add(('共同参与', j, tuple(sorted(U))))
                for (p, x, c) in host.comps(w, y0, s):
                    if x in E and abs(total_comp(host, w2, s, p, x) - comp_of(host, w, y0, s, p, x)) > eps:
                        hyper.add(('历史条件', p + '@' + x, tuple(sorted(set(U) | {x}))))
    covered = set()
    for U in tested:
        for a in U:
            for b in U:
                covered.add((a, b))
    unknown = [(a, b) for a, b in combinations(sorted(E), 2) if (a, b) not in covered]
    return sorted(hyper), unknown

def basins(F, chain):
    """有限全序链 chain 上的映射 F（dict）。返回 {不动点: [其吸引域（极限等于该点的原像）]}。"""
    out = {}
    for d in chain:
        x, seen = d, set()
        while F[x] != x:
            if x in seen:
                x = None
                break
            seen.add(x)
            x = F[x]
        out.setdefault(x, []).append(d)
    return out

def order_rel(a1, a2):
    return (a1 > a2) - (a1 < a2)

def order_conflicts(pairs):
    """返回 q 与 e 的序关系不一致的点对（含等号错配两个方向）。"""
    bad = []
    for i in range(len(pairs)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(pairs)):
            (q1, e1), (q2, e2) = pairs[i], pairs[j]
            if order_rel(q1, q2) != order_rel(e1, e2):
                bad.append((pairs[i], pairs[j]))
    return bad

def fstr(x):
    if isinstance(x, Fr):
        return str(x.numerator) if x.denominator == 1 else '%d/%d' % (x.numerator, x.denominator)
    return str(x)

def avail_at(host, states, k, T, eps, k_form=None):
    """拍 k 的可用承担位：拍 k 的拍前态 states[k] 中取向深度 > eps 的 T 中位。
    states[k] 是此前各拍写入的结果，故其越阈意味着在拍 k 之前已留下；若越阈始自 states[0]（起点即越阈），
    其形成须另有记录（〔封三〕ENT 的对应物），本函数不代证；程序在此只验数值上的「已留下」。
    T 的窗末成员身份不用于此判断。"""
    return [x for x in T if depth(host, states[k], x) > eps]

def hc_status(host, w, s, proc, x, U_av, eps, fracs=FRACS):
    """历史条件在一拍一过程上的三种判值（只改可用位 U_av 的合法设定族，含联合设定）：
    '有效差异已获证'：某设定使总分项差 > eps；'函数支持已立'：某设定使总分项差 ≠ 0 而无一 > eps；
    '有效差异未获证'：所测设定族内总分项差恒为 0（这只是有限设定族内的读数，不是完整否定）。"""
    best = None
    nonzero = False
    for (U, f, w2) in settings(host, w, U_av, fracs, joint=True):
        A, D, tot = branches(host, w, w2, s, proc, x)
        if abs(tot) > eps:
            return '有效差异已获证', (U, f, A, D, tot)
        if tot != 0:
            nonzero = True
            best = (U, f, A, D, tot)
    if nonzero:
        return '函数支持已立', best
    return '有效差异未获证', None

def hdyn_cert(host, states, sigs, T, eps, fracs=FRACS):
    """同拍、同一过程上的 Act 与历史差异：Act = 该拍该过程在 x ∈ T 上的总分项 |c| > eps；
    设定只改该拍的可用承担位（avail_at）。返回 (判值, 见证)；判值 ∈ {'1', '函数支持已立', '未获证'}。
    本函数只验模型的结构依赖与数值差（模型模式）；实际物质途径与作用认证另由申报承担。"""
    status = '未获证'
    wit = None
    for k, s in enumerate(sigs):
        w = states[k]
        Uav = avail_at(host, states, k, T, eps)
        if not Uav:
            continue
        y = host.act(w, s)
        seen = set()
        for (p, x, _c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
            if x not in T or (p, x) in seen:
                continue
            seen.add((p, x))
            c = comp_of(host, w, y, s, p, x)
            if abs(c) <= eps:
                continue
            st, wt = hc_status(host, w, s, p, x, Uav, eps, fracs)
            if st == '有效差异已获证':
                return '1', (k, s, p, x, c, Uav) + wt
            if st == '函数支持已立' and status == '未获证':
                status, wit = '函数支持已立', (k, s, p, x, c, Uav) + wt
    return status, wit

def branches_rev(host, w, w2, s, proc, x):
    """另一申报次序的望远镜分解：先改直读（活动保持原值），再换活动。返回 (D″, A″, 总差)。"""
    y, y2 = host.act(w, s), host.act(w2, s)
    c0 = comp_of(host, w, y, s, proc, x)
    cD = comp_of(host, w2, y, s, proc, x)
    c2 = comp_of(host, w2, y2, s, proc, x)
    return cD - c0, c2 - cD, c2 - c0
~~~

A.8　jxs\_core.py　上游冻结源码：〔十八〕《迹应集合变换数理式》v1.0 附录 A.1

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
《迹应集合变换数理式》v1.0 附录程序：运行系统核心库。
对应正文第三至五节：完整状态 σ = (d, r)；读出律 Φ；写入分项；残留律 P；两套分辨率语义；
势、受影响集、作用集；精确动力同一；线性定常闭环的永隐判据；F 层编译与两种历史条件读法。
纪律：只用标准库；一切数值为 fractions.Fraction；无随机数。
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from itertools import product

class Host:
    """申报宿载模型 𝔐（D18-1）。
    positions：带身份标签的结构位列表（X̂，可含存在变量所控的潜在位）。
    read(d, r, s) -> dict：全活动 y（D18-7）。
    comps(d, y, s) -> dict pos -> list[(过程标识, 分项 c)]：写入律的合法逐过程分解（D18-1）。
    post(d_new, d_old) -> dict：申报的后处理（如夹限、存在位生成）；无则恒等。
    resid(r, y) -> r'：残留律；无残留时 r 恒为 None。
    """
    def __init__(self, positions, read, comps, post=None, resid=None):
        self.positions = list(positions)
        self.read = read
        self.comps = comps
        self.post = post or (lambda dn, do: dn)
        self.resid = resid or (lambda r, y: r)

def step(host, d, r, s, eps=F(0), sem="raw"):
    """一拍运行（运行方程）。sem = 'raw'：原机制语义，全部分项入账；'booked'：有效入账语义，只入账 |c| > eps。
    返回 (y, d', r', 记录)；记录含各位分项、Act⁰、Act^ε、Aff⁰、Aff^ε。"""
    y = host.read(d, r, s)
    cs = host.comps(d, y, s)
    dn = dict(d)
    for x, lst in cs.items():
        if sem == "raw":
            dn[x] = d[x] + sum(c for _, c in lst)
        else:
            dn[x] = d[x] + sum(c for _, c in lst if abs(c) > eps)
    dn = host.post(dn, d)
    rn = host.resid(r, y)
    act0 = {x for x, lst in cs.items() if any(c != 0 for _, c in lst)}
    acte = {x for x, lst in cs.items() if any(abs(c) > eps for _, c in lst)}
    aff0 = {x for x in host.positions if dn.get(x, F(0)) != d.get(x, F(0))}
    affe = {x for x in host.positions if abs(dn.get(x, F(0)) - d.get(x, F(0))) > eps}
    return y, dn, rn, {"comps": cs, "Act0": act0, "Acte": acte, "Aff0": aff0, "Affe": affe}

def run(host, d, r, hist, eps=F(0), sem="raw"):
    ys = []
    for s in hist:
        y, d, r, _ = step(host, d, r, s, eps, sem)
        ys.append(y)
    return ys, d, r

def potential(host, d, r, signals):
    """势 Φ_σ（D18-6）：带标签信号类上的全活动响应。"""
    return {s: host.read(d, r, s) for s in signals}

def exact_dyn_same(host, s1, s2, alphabet, L, eps=F(0), sem="raw"):
    """精确动力同一（D18-14）在长度 ≤ L 的全部史上的有限核验；返回 (是否, 史数)。
    eps 与 sem 贯穿运行与比较：缺省为原机制语义（sem = "raw"）；有效入账语义须显式传入 sem = "booked" 与 ε。"""
    n = 0
    for k in range(L + 1):
        for h in product(alphabet, repeat=k):
            n += 1
            y1, _, _ = run(host, s1[0], s1[1], h, eps, sem)
            y2, _, _ = run(host, s2[0], s2[1], h, eps, sem)
            if y1 != y2:
                return False, n
    return True, n

def booked_sum(lst, eps):
    """纯数值筛后写入 W^ε_num 的分项和：只计 |c| > ε 的分项（step 的 sem = "booked" 即此算子）。"""
    return sum(c for _, c in lst if abs(c) > eps)

def booked_sum_F(lst, eps, cert):
    """F 层认证筛后写入 W^ε_F 的分项和：只计 |c| > ε 且 cert_eff = 1 的分项（〔封二〕4.2）。cert：{过程: 1/0/'⊥'}，缺省 1。"""
    return sum(c for rho, c in lst if abs(c) > eps and cert.get(rho, 1) == 1)

def pairing_ok(cs_a, cs_b, pi):
    """过程身份配对的充分条件（定理 18-2（一）有效入账款）：对每一位 x，状态 a 在 x 上的 (过程, 分项) 多重集
    与状态 b 在 π(x) 上的相同（逐分项保数值；阈值 ε 两侧同一）。这保证纯数值筛后写入 W^ε_num 交换；
    F 层认证筛后写入 W^ε_F 另须配对两侧的认证状态相同（cert_pairing_ok）。"""
    keys = set(cs_a) | {k for k in pi}
    for x in keys:
        la = sorted(cs_a.get(x, []), key=lambda t: (str(t[0]), t[1]))
        lb = sorted(cs_b.get(pi.get(x, x), []), key=lambda t: (str(t[0]), t[1]))
        if la != lb:
            return False
    return True

def cert_pairing_ok(cs_a, cert_a, cert_b, pi):
    """配对两侧的作用认证状态相同：位 x 上过程 ρ 的 cert_eff 等于位 π(x) 上同名过程的 cert_eff。cert：{(过程, 位): 值}。"""
    for x, lst in cs_a.items():
        for rho, _ in lst:
            if cert_a.get((rho, x), 1) != cert_b.get((rho, pi.get(x, x)), 1):
                return False
    return True

def mat_mul(A, B):
    return [[sum(A[i][k] * B[k][j] for k in range(len(B))) for j in range(len(B[0]))] for i in range(len(A))]

def hidden_forever_lti(A, C, delta):
    """线性定常闭环 Δ' = AΔ、读数 CΔ：Cayley–Hamilton，检 k = 0..n−1 即为全称判据（定理 18-7（四））。"""
    v = [[x] for x in delta]
    for _ in range(len(A)):
        if any(row[0] != 0 for row in mat_mul(C, v)):
            return False
        v = mat_mul(A, v)
    return True

# ---- F 层编译与两种历史条件读法（定理 18-4） ----
def f_component(law, s):
    """〔封二〕D12 的三类数值律：('const', b) / ('lin', b, k, R) / ('bil', b, k, j1, j2)。"""
    if law[0] == "const":
        return law[1]
    if law[0] == "lin":
        _, b, k, R = law
        return b + k * sum(s[j] for j in R)
    _, b, k, j1, j2 = law
    return b + k * s[j1] * s[j2]

def f_supp_act(law, s):
    """〔封二〕D15 的函数支持（数学非零，与 ε 无关）。"""
    if law[0] == "const":
        return set()
    if law[0] == "lin":
        return set(law[3]) if law[2] != 0 else set()
    _, b, k, j1, j2 = law
    if k == 0:
        return set()
    out = set()
    if s[j2] != 0:
        out.add(j1)
    if s[j1] != 0:
        out.add(j2)
    return out

def histcond_F(law, s, T, eps, formed):
    """〔封二〕D17、D18 的 HistCond_F（路径认证取 1、cert_read 取 1）：可用位 = Supp_act ∩ T 中 s > ε 且已形成者。"""
    avail = {i for i in f_supp_act(law, s) & set(T) if s[i] > eps and i in formed}
    return 1 if avail else 0

def histcond_diff(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """单设对照的数值差读法：存在只改 T 中一个已形成、深度 > ε 的位的合法对照，总分项差 > ε（返回 1/0）。
    这是〔十七〕定义 6′（丙）款的数值部分在单设子协议上的检验，不是完整证书。contrasts：合法取值表 {位: [值…]}。"""
    base = f_component(law, s)
    pos = [i for i in T if i in formed and s[i] > eps]
    for i in pos:
        for v in contrasts.get(i, []):
            s2 = dict(s); s2[i] = v
            if abs(f_component(law, s2) - base) > eps:
                return 1
    return 0

# ---- 带路径与读取认证的 HistCond_F；联合对照；两列原始判断；F 层入账（含 cert_eff） ----
def histcond_F_full(law, s, T, eps, formed, path_from, cert_read=1):
    """〔封二〕D18：HistCond_F = [Avail_act ≠ ∅] ∧ cert_read ∧ Path({(i,t) : i ∈ Avail_act}, m)。
    path_from(起点集) -> 1/0/'⊥'：申报证据路径图上自该起点集到本机制事件的路径状态（D14）。"""
    avail = {i for i in f_supp_act(law, s) & set(T) if s[i] > eps and i in formed}
    if not avail:
        return 0
    if cert_read == 0:
        return 0
    p = path_from(frozenset(avail))
    if p == 0:
        return 0
    if cert_read == "⊥" or p == "⊥":
        return "⊥"
    return 1

def histcond_diff_joint(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """笛卡尔闭包枚举（显式申报联合合法对照族为逐位候选值的笛卡尔闭包时使用）：对 T 中已形成、深度 > ε 的位的任意非空子集同时取合法值。
    返回 '数值差越阈' 或 '数值差未越阈'（只是〔十七〕定义 6′（丙）款的数值部分；后者亦不是否定）。"""
    from itertools import combinations, product as prod
    base = f_component(law, s)
    pos = [i for i in T if i in formed and s[i] > eps]
    for k in range(1, len(pos) + 1):
        for sub in combinations(pos, k):
            for vals in prod(*[contrasts.get(i, []) for i in sub]):
                s2 = dict(s)
                for i, v in zip(sub, vals):
                    s2[i] = v
                if abs(f_component(law, s2) - base) > eps:
                    return "数值差越阈"
    return "数值差未越阈"

def readback_hist(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts, path_from, cert_read=1):
    """两列原始判断：(F 层函数支持之值, 联合对照下的数值差判断)。"""
    hf = histcond_F_full(law, s, T, eps, formed, path_from, cert_read)
    hd = histcond_diff_joint(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts)
    return ("函数支持已立" if hf == 1 else ("函数支持未定" if hf == "⊥" else "函数支持未立")), hd

def f_layer_update(comps, eps):
    """〔封二〕4.2 的 F 层入账：只计 Act_F = [|c| > ε] ∧ cert_eff = 1 的分项。comps：[(c, cert_eff)]。"""
    return sum(c for c, ce in comps if abs(c) > eps and ce == 1)

def raw_update(comps):
    return sum(c for c, _ in comps)

# ---- D18 三值合取；回读原子与三栏摘要；实际方程下的 F 层对照 ----
def kleene_and(vals):
    """三值合取：任一项为 0 得 0；无 0 而有 ⊥ 得 ⊥；全为 1 得 1。"""
    if any(v == 0 for v in vals):
        return 0
    if any(v == "⊥" for v in vals):
        return "⊥"
    return 1

def max_contrast_diff(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """笛卡尔闭包上的最大差：只在申报的联合合法对照族恰为逐位候选值的笛卡尔闭包时使用（显式申报，不作缺省）。"""
    from itertools import combinations, product as prod
    base = f_component(law, s)
    pos = [i for i in T if i in formed and s[i] > eps]
    best = F(0)
    for k in range(1, len(pos) + 1):
        for sub in combinations(pos, k):
            for vals in prod(*[contrasts.get(i, []) for i in sub]):
                s2 = dict(s)
                for i, v in zip(sub, vals):
                    s2[i] = v
                best = max(best, abs(f_component(law, s2) - base))
    return best

def kleene_or(vals):
    """三值析取：任一项为 1 得 1；无 1 而有 ⊥ 得 ⊥；全为 0（或空）得 0。"""
    if any(v == 1 for v in vals):
        return 1
    if any(v == "⊥" for v in vals):
        return "⊥"
    return 0

def contrast_family(contrasts):
    """规范化对照族（定理 18-4 引言）。
    contrasts 为 {位: [值…]}：单设简写，每位每值各成一条单设对照，不自动生成联合对照；申报覆盖记「完整」。
    contrasts 为 {"对照": [...], "覆盖": "完整"/"不完整"}：冻结的对照集合，每条为 {"id": 名, "set": {位: 值}, "证据": {...}}；
      联合对照须作为一条显式列出（或由联合合法性谓词生成后列出）。
    返回 (对照列表, 覆盖)。"""
    if "对照" in contrasts:
        fam = [dict(id=k.get("id", "对照%d" % n), set=dict(k["set"]), 证据=dict(k.get("证据", {})))
               for n, k in enumerate(contrasts["对照"])]
        ids = [k["id"] for k in fam]
        if len(set(ids)) != len(ids):
            raise ValueError("对照身份重复")
        return fam, contrasts.get("覆盖", "完整")
    fam = []
    for i in sorted(contrasts, key=str):
        for v in contrasts[i]:
            fam.append(dict(id="%s→%s" % (i, v), set={i: v}, 证据={}))
    return fam, "完整"

def numeric_diff_family(law, s, T, eps, formed, fam, cover):
    """数值差项：只计合于〔十七〕定义 6′（乙）款的对照（只改 T 中已形成、深度 > ε 的位）。
    取值「数值差越阈」「数值差非零未越阈」「数值差为零」；无一越阈而申报覆盖不完整时记「对照域覆盖不足」。"""
    base = f_component(law, s)
    m = F(0)
    for k in fam:
        act_set = {i: v for i, v in k["set"].items() if v != s[i]}
        if all(i in T and i in formed and s[i] > eps for i in act_set):
            s2 = dict(s); s2.update(act_set)
            m = max(m, abs(f_component(law, s2) - base))
    if m > eps:
        return "数值差越阈"
    if cover != "完整":
        return "对照域覆盖不足"
    return "数值差非零未越阈" if m > 0 else "数值差为零"

def numeric_diff(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """数值差项：〔十七〕定义 6′（丙）款的数值部分，不是完整证书（对照族按 contrast_family 规范化）。"""
    fam, cover = contrast_family(contrasts)
    return numeric_diff_family(law, s, T, eps, formed, fam, cover)

def readback_atoms(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts, path_from, cert_read=1, cert_eff=1, evidence=None,
                   independent=None):
    """定理 18-4 的回读原子与三栏摘要。
    对照族：按 contrast_family 规范化；以同一拍、同一过程、同一目标与对照身份逐条计算差与认证，再对完整见证取析取；
    认证绑定到取得越阈差的那一条对照，不跨对照拼接。
    原子：数值支持 Avail、读取认证 cert_read、路径 Path、HistCond_F（〔封二〕D18 原合取）、Act_F（含 cert_eff）、
    数值差（numeric_diff_family），逐条对照的明细，以及〔十七〕定义 6′ 四款的逐项证据（同拍、承担位、途径、作用）。
    evidence：同一 F 事件的共同证据 {键: 1/0/'⊥'}，申报为对各条对照共同适用；对照自带的「证据」逐款优先。各款缺省：
    同拍由对照的构造给出，记 1；承担位按（乙）款逐条判定（只改 T 中已形成、深度 > ε 的位记 1，否则 0）；途径绑定该条
    对照实际改动的承担位——自这些起点到本事件的路径为 1 时记 1，否则 '⊥'（路径为 0 不排除另外的物质途径；另申报的
    合法实际途径须独立带证书补足）；聚合 Path 只作独立原子，不替代逐条途径；作用取 Act_F。
    对照明细逐条记 (对照身份, |Δ|, 该条见证, 该条实际四款)；「定义6′四款」取取证对照的四款（无取证对照时取首条）。
    「证书状态」：有取证对照时记其身份；否则记已列对照证书析取之值，申报覆盖不完整时另记「申报域尚未覆盖」。
    作用一款的合并：F 原子 Act_F 是同一事件的作用证据。
      Act_F = 0（明确否证）：外部声称不得覆盖；外部声称 1 时记「证据冲突」并按原否证阻断，该款取 0。
      Act_F = 1：外部声称 0 时同记「证据冲突」，该款取 0（保守阻断）；外部声称 1 或未给时取 1。
      Act_F = ⊥（未证）：外部显式认证可补足（取其值，来源记为「显式认证」）；外部显式给 ⊥ 时来源记「显式未证」；
      未给时来源记「未证」，该款为 ⊥。
    independent：不同机制或独立 R 层见证，须为 {事件身份, 模式, 认证依据, 作用}；单列于原子「独立见证」，
      不并入本 F 事件的四款、完整证书与摘要；字段不全时记「独立见证不完整」。
    完整证书 = 对各条对照的 [|Δ| > ε ∧ 四款] 取三值析取。摘要按〔十七〕定义 6′ 的判值三分：完整证书为 1 记
    「有效差异已获证」；否则数值差非零未越阈（申报覆盖完整），或 HistCond_F = 1，记「函数支持已立」；其余记
    「有效差异未获证」。摘要不覆盖原子；冲突、未证与否证分别见原子「证据冲突」「作用来源」。"""
    evidence = dict(evidence or {})
    fam, cover = contrast_family(contrasts)
    avail = {i for i in f_supp_act(law, s) & set(T) if s[i] > eps and i in formed}
    path = path_from(frozenset(avail)) if avail else 0
    hf = histcond_F_full(law, s, T, eps, formed, path_from, cert_read)
    c = f_component(law, s)
    act = kleene_and([1 if abs(c) > eps else 0, cert_eff])
    nd = numeric_diff_family(law, s, T, eps, formed, fam, cover)
    conflicts = []
    ext = evidence.get("作用")
    if act == 0:
        act_item, src = 0, "F 原子否证"
        if ext == 1:
            conflicts.append("作用：F 原子 Act_F = 0（明确否证）与外部声称 1 冲突，按原否证阻断")
    elif act == 1:
        if ext == 0:
            act_item, src = 0, "外部否证"
            conflicts.append("作用：F 原子 Act_F = 1 与外部声称 0 冲突，保守阻断")
        else:
            act_item, src = 1, "F 原子"
    else:
        if ext is None:
            act_item, src = "⊥", "未证"
        elif ext == "⊥":
            act_item, src = "⊥", "显式未证"
        else:
            act_item, src = ext, "显式认证"
    detail, certs, witness, wfour, first_four = [], [], None, None, None
    for k in fam:
        ev = k["证据"]
        # 实际差集：只保留取值确与拍前值不同的位（设回拍前值的设置不算改动）
        act_set = {i: v for i, v in k["set"].items() if v != s[i]}
        legal = all(i in T and i in formed and s[i] > eps for i in act_set)
        s2 = dict(s); s2.update(act_set)
        dk = abs(f_component(law, s2) - c)
        # 途径绑定该条对照实际改动的承担位：自这些起点到本事件的路径；聚合 Path 不替代
        bearers = frozenset(i for i in act_set if i in avail)
        p_k = path_from(bearers) if bearers else 0
        route_k = ev.get("途径", evidence.get("途径", 1 if p_k == 1 else "⊥"))
        four_k = {"同拍": ev.get("同拍", evidence.get("同拍", 1)),
                  "承担位": ev.get("承担位", evidence.get("承担位", 1)) if legal else 0,
                  "途径": route_k, "作用": act_item}
        ck = kleene_and([1 if dk > eps else 0] + list(four_k.values()))
        detail.append((k["id"], dk, ck, dict(four_k)))
        if first_four is None:
            first_four = dict(four_k)
        certs.append(ck)
        if ck == 1 and witness is None:
            witness, wfour = k["id"], dict(four_k)
    cert = kleene_or(certs)
    four = wfour if witness is not None else (first_four if first_four is not None else
           {"同拍": evidence.get("同拍", 1), "承担位": 0, "途径": "⊥", "作用": act_item})
    status = ("取证对照「%s」" % witness if witness is not None else
              "已列对照证书析取为 %s" % cert + ("；申报域尚未覆盖" if cover != "完整" else ""))
    atoms = {"Avail": 1 if avail else 0, "cert_read": cert_read, "Path": path, "HistCond_F": hf,
             "Act_F": act, "数值差": nd, "定义6′四款": four, "作用来源": src, "证据冲突": conflicts,
             "对照明细": detail, "取证对照": witness, "完整证书": cert, "覆盖": cover, "证书状态": status}
    if independent is not None:
        need = ("事件身份", "模式", "认证依据", "作用")
        atoms["独立见证"] = dict(independent) if all(k in independent for k in need) else "独立见证不完整"
    if cert == 1:
        col = "有效差异已获证"
    elif hf == 1 or nd == "数值差非零未越阈":
        col = "函数支持已立"
    else:
        col = "有效差异未获证"
    return atoms, col

def f_post_match(post, d, raw_sum, f_sum):
    """定理 18-4（四）（甲）的实际方程比较：原机制一拍 post(d + Σ原) 与 F 层一拍 d + ΣF。"""
    return post(d + raw_sum) == d + f_sum

# ---- J₁₈：经〔封二〕F 层求值器的校验、轨迹复现与资格（定理 18-4（四）） ----
def _tri(v):
    return {1: True, 0: False, "⊥": None, True: True, False: False, None: None}[v]

def _untri(v):
    return {True: 1, False: 0, None: "⊥"}[v]

def j18_spec(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links=(), identity=1, trace_history=1, closed_graph=None):
    """把本篇一次运行的申报事件编译为〔封二〕附录 A.1 的 Spec（原始事件、认证、时间与来源显式传入，不压缩）。
    events：每条为
      {"类": "脉冲", "名": 事件标识, "拍": t, "目标": 位, "量": a, "界外": True/False, "发生": 1/0/'⊥'}，或
      {"类": "过程", "名": 事件标识, "拍": t, "目标": 位, "载体在内": True/False, "律": 'constant'/'linear'/'bilinear',
       "读取": (位…), "系数": k, "偏置": b, "cert_eff": 1/0/'⊥', "cert_read": 1/0/'⊥'}。
    links：[(源结点, 目标结点, 认证)]，结点为 'state:位:拍' 或事件标识。
    closed_graph：证据图是否闭合，须由证据协议申报（True/False）；未申报（None）时不编译。"""
    if closed_graph not in (True, False):
        raise ValueError("证据图是否闭合未申报")
    import field_final as FF
    pulses = tuple(FF.Pulse(e["名"], e["拍"], e["目标"], e["量"], e.get("界外", True), _tri(e.get("发生", 1)))
                   for e in events if e["类"] == "脉冲")
    procs = tuple(FF.Process(e["名"], e["拍"], e["目标"], e["载体在内"], e["律"], tuple(e.get("读取", ())),
                             e.get("系数", F(0)), e.get("偏置", F(0)), _tri(e.get("cert_eff", 1)), _tri(e.get("cert_read", 1)))
                  for e in events if e["类"] == "过程")
    lk = tuple(FF.Link(a, b, _tri(c)) for a, b, c in links)
    return FF.Spec(tuple(sites), tuple(initial), tuple(window), pulses, procs, lk, _tri(identity), _tri(trace_history),
                   closed_graph, eps, tuple(T))

def j18_raw_run(sites, initial, window, events):
    """本篇原机制语义下的同一运行（post 恒等）：每拍各过程按其律读拍前态求分项，发生的脉冲计入，全部分项入账。
    参与计算的一切数值先按〔封二〕的 exact 规范化（十进制小数按其十进制表示取精确有理数），与上游求值器同一解释。"""
    from field_final import exact
    st = {x: exact(v) for x, v in zip(sites, initial)}
    traj = []
    for t in range(window[0] - 1, window[-1] + 1):
        before = dict(st)
        add = {x: F(0) for x in sites}
        for e in events:
            if e["拍"] != t:
                continue
            if e["类"] == "脉冲":
                if e.get("发生", 1) == 1:
                    add[e["目标"]] += exact(e["量"])
            else:
                rd = e.get("读取", ()); k = exact(e.get("系数", F(0))); b = exact(e.get("偏置", F(0)))
                v = b if e["律"] == "constant" else (b + k * sum(before[q] for q in rd) if e["律"] == "linear"
                                                     else b + k * before[rd[0]] * before[rd[1]])
                add[e["目标"]] += v
        st = {x: before[x] + add[x] for x in sites}
        if t in window:
            traj.append(dict(st))
    return traj

def j18_check(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links=(), identity=1, trace_history=1, closed_graph=None):
    """定理 18-4（四）的三条件，经〔封二〕求值器（field_final.check、run）判值：
    （甲）可生成 F 候选事件表：j18_spec 编译成功（证据图是否闭合由证据协议申报），且 field_final.check 按 D20 接受原始认证与原始事件；
         D20 拒绝时（如作用认证未定，零分项亦然）不运行 F，另报 R 层待定；
    （乙）复现原运行数值轨迹：原机制同一运行的各拍后态与 F 运行报告的各拍后态逐位相等；
    （丙）保见证与资格：由 field_final.run 求 D19 的 Trace_F、Part_F、HDyn_F、Q_F 与逐事件原子。"""
    import field_final as FF
    try:
        spec = j18_spec(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links, identity, trace_history, closed_graph)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "拒绝：%s" % exc, "R": "须由证据协议申报后再求值", "事件表": 0, "轨迹": "未运行",
                "Trace_F": "未运行", "Part_F": "未运行", "HDyn_F": "未运行", "Q_F": "未运行", "事件": []}
    try:
        FF.check(spec)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "拒绝：%s" % exc, "R": "R 层待定（另按 R 层事件见证求值）", "事件表": 0, "轨迹": "未运行",
                "Trace_F": "未运行", "Part_F": "未运行", "HDyn_F": "未运行", "Q_F": "未运行", "事件": []}
    try:
        out = FF.run(spec)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "拒绝（运行中越出定义域）：%s" % exc, "R": "R 层待定（另按 R 层事件见证求值）", "事件表": 1,
                "轨迹": "未运行", "Trace_F": "未运行", "Part_F": "未运行", "HDyn_F": "未运行", "Q_F": "未运行", "事件": []}
    f_traj = [rep["after"] for rep in out["reports"]]
    raw = j18_raw_run(sites, initial, window, events)
    traj = 1 if [{x: F(v) for x, v in a.items()} for a in f_traj] == raw else 0
    evs = [dict(id=e["id"], c=e["delta"], Act_F=_untri(e["act"]), Cond_F=_untri(e["cond"]), HistCond_F=_untri(e["histcond"]))
           for rep in out["reports"] for e in rep["events"]]
    return {"D20": "接受", "R": "—", "事件表": 1, "轨迹": traj, "Trace_F": _untri(out["trace"]), "Part_F": _untri(out["part"]),
            "HDyn_F": _untri(out["hdyn"]), "Q_F": _untri(out["Q"]), "事件": evs, "F 轨迹": f_traj, "原机制轨迹": raw}

def nonneg_encode(d):
    """签名深度进入 F 层的非负编码：d = d⁺ − d⁻，d⁺、d⁻ ≥ 0；返回 (编码, 回读函数)。"""
    enc = {}
    for x, v in d.items():
        enc[(x, "+")] = v if v > 0 else F(0)
        enc[(x, "−")] = -v if v < 0 else F(0)
    return enc, (lambda e: {x: e[(x, "+")] - e[(x, "−")] for x in d})
~~~

A.9　field\_final.py　上游冻结源码：〔封二〕《场具化数理式》v1.0 附录 A.1（A.8 的依赖闭合所需）

~~~python
"""场具化数理式 v1.0 附录 A：F 层（有限可分层）求值器。
形式模拟，不是物质因果认证。
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from math import isfinite
from collections import defaultdict
from fractions import Fraction
Tri = bool | None
def exact(v):
    """输入数值按其十进制表示取精确有理数；F 层全部运算在有理数上精确进行。"""
    return Fraction(repr(v)) if isinstance(v, float) else Fraction(v)
def tri_and(*xs):
    if False in xs:return False
    if None in xs:return None
    return True
def tri_or(xs):
    xs=list(xs)
    if True in xs:return True
    if None in xs:return None
    return False
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Pulse:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; amount:float; external:bool=True; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Process:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; carrier_inside:bool; law:str
    reads:tuple[str,...]=(); coefficient:float=0.; bias:float=0.
    certified_effect:Tri=True; certified_read:Tri=True
    # rule: constant, linear (sum of read sites), bilinear (two sites)
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Link:
    source:str; target:str; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Spec:
    sites:tuple[str,...]; initial:tuple[float,...]; window:tuple[int,...]
    pulses:tuple[Pulse,...]; processes:tuple[Process,...]
    links:tuple[Link,...]=(); identity:Tri=True; trace_history:Tri=True
    closed_graph:bool=True; epsilon:float=1e-9
    declared_T:tuple[str,...]|None=None

def _num(v, nonneg=False):
    # 合法数值：整数、有理数（Fraction）或有限的十进制浮点数
    if isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, (int, float, Fraction)): return False
    if isinstance(v, float) and not isfinite(v): return False
    return (not nonneg) or v >= 0
def check(x):
    if not x.sites or len(set(x.sites))!=len(x.sites):raise ValueError('sites')
    if x.declared_T is None or not x.declared_T or len(set(x.declared_T))!=len(x.declared_T) or any(s not in x.sites for s in x.declared_T):raise ValueError('declared T')
    if not x.window or tuple(range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+1))!=x.window:raise ValueError('window')
    if len(x.initial)!=len(x.sites) or not all(_num(z,True) for z in x.initial):raise ValueError('initial')
    if not _num(x.epsilon,True):raise ValueError('epsilon')
    if x.identity not in (True,False,None) or x.trace_history not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('identity')
    names=set()
    for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes):
        if not e.name or e.name in names:raise ValueError('event id')
        names.add(e.name)
        if e.step<x.window[0]-1 or e.step>x.window[-1]:raise ValueError('event time')
        if e.target not in x.sites:raise ValueError('target')
        if isinstance(e,Pulse):
            if not _num(e.amount,True):raise ValueError('pulse amount')
            # certified 在 F 中是脉冲「是否实际发生」的认证：1 发生，0 不发生；⊥ 不属 F 单轨迹定义域，须在 R 中按分支枚举
            if e.certified is None:raise ValueError('F_UNDECIDED_PULSE: enumerate occurrence branches in R')
            if e.certified not in (True,False):raise ValueError('pulse evidence')
        else:
            if e.law not in ('constant','linear','bilinear'):raise ValueError('law')
            if any(s not in x.sites for s in e.reads):raise ValueError('read sites')
            if e.law=='bilinear' and len(e.reads)!=2:raise ValueError('bilinear arity')
            if e.law=='linear' and not e.reads:raise ValueError('linear reads')
            if e.law=='constant' and e.reads:raise ValueError('constant reads')
            if not _num(e.coefficient) or not _num(e.bias):raise ValueError('params')
            # D20：作用认证须已决定；作用未定的机制不属 F 定义域（零分项亦然）
            if e.certified_effect not in (True,False):raise ValueError('undecided mechanism effect')
            if e.certified_read not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('evidence')
    # All trace nodes are snapshots at integer t; process nodes at integer t+0.5.
    # Strict temporal order prevents fabricated same-step read-after-write.
    times={f'state:{s}:{t}':float(t) for s in x.sites for t in range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+2)}
    times.update({e.name:float(e.step)+0.5 for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes)})
    for link in x.links:
        if link.source not in times or link.target not in times:raise ValueError('link endpoint')
        if times[link.source]>=times[link.target]:raise ValueError('noncausal link')
        if link.certified not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('link evidence')
    return times
def path_status(links,starts,target,closed=True):
    adjacency=defaultdict(list)
    for l in links:adjacency[l.source].append(l)
    def reachable(allow):
        seen=set(starts);queue=list(starts)
        while queue:
            u=queue.pop()
            for l in adjacency[u]:
                if allow(l.certified) and l.target not in seen:
                    seen.add(l.target);queue.append(l.target)
        return target in seen
    if reachable(lambda w:w is True):return True
    if reachable(lambda w:w is not False):return None
    return False if closed else None
def run(x):
    check(x)
    eps=exact(x.epsilon)
    state={s:exact(v) for s,v in zip(x.sites,x.initial)}
    # For each site, certified external history must be identified as event, not inferred from nonzero state.
    formation=defaultdict(list); history_snapshots=defaultdict(list)
    signal_marks=defaultdict(list)  # 界外成因：已认证、有效的界外作用分项（脉冲或界外载体过程）在该位留下的改变
    part=[];hdyn=[];reports=[]
    for t in range(x.window[0]-1,x.window[-1]+1):
        before=dict(state); contributions=defaultdict(list);events=[]
        # Pulse first as an event, but processes read the prior snapshot, not pulse-modified value.
        for p in x.pulses:
            if p.step!=t:continue
            # 只计已认证发生且有效的脉冲；确认不发生者不计贡献、不登记形成与成因
            a=exact(p.amount)
            if p.certified is True and a>eps:
                contributions[p.target].append((p.name,a))
                formation[p.target].append((p.name,t,p.external))
                if p.external:signal_marks[p.target].append((p.name,t))
        for m in x.processes:
            if m.step!=t:continue
            k=exact(m.coefficient); b=exact(m.bias)
            if m.law=='constant':value=b
            elif m.law=='linear':value=b+k*sum(before[s] for s in m.reads)
            else:value=b+k*before[m.reads[0]]*before[m.reads[1]]
            is_action=tri_and(abs(value)>eps,m.certified_effect)
            # Declared reads do not establish a genuine dependence: compute the law's support.
            support=()
            # 组织条件的支持：数值律依赖读取位，或纯读取过程（分项为零）；读取认证另作合取，未定不预先截为假
            # 函数支持按数学上的非零偏导判定（系数与伙伴读数精确不为零），不与有效改变阈值 ε 混用
            if m.law=='linear' and (k!=0 or abs(value)<=eps): support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            # For every element in the declared T, historical formation must precede the read.
            active_T=set(x.declared_T)
            available=[s for s in support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            is_cond=tri_and(bool(available),m.certified_read)
            # No automatic path from a read-site to an act. Explicit event links are required.
            old_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in available]
            route=path_status(x.links,old_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if old_nodes else False
            # Action provenance must be supported by a nonzero functional dependence,
            # not merely a certified read that has no effect on the action.
            action_support=()
            if m.law=='linear' and k!=0:action_support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                action_support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            action_available=[s for s in action_support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            action_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in action_available]
            action_route=path_status(x.links,action_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if action_nodes else False
            is_hist=tri_and(bool(action_available),m.certified_read,action_route)
            if is_action is True:
                contributions[m.target].append((m.name,value))
                # 新元素的形成：B 内载体者为场内续行形成，界外载体者为信号形成
                if value>eps and before[m.target]<=eps:
                    formation[m.target].append((m.name,t,not m.carrier_inside))
                # 界外载体过程的有效分项，不论新旧元素、不论正负，都是信号留下的改变
                if not m.carrier_inside:
                    signal_marks[m.target].append((m.name,t))
            elif is_action is None: # 防御性检查：作用认证未定已由 check() 排除于定义域之外
                raise ValueError('unresolved effect cannot determine numeric trajectory')
            if t in x.window:
                if m.carrier_inside:part.append(is_cond)
                hdyn.append(tri_and(m.target in active_T,is_action,is_hist))
            events.append({'id':m.name,'delta':value,'act':is_action,'cond':is_cond,'histcond':is_hist,'route':route})
        for s in x.sites:
            new=before[s]+sum(v for _,v in contributions[s])
            # 深度不得为负：精确更新值小于零者越出定义域，不截零、不设容差
            if new < 0:raise ValueError('negative trace depth')
            state[s]=new
            if state[s]>eps and formation[s]:history_snapshots[s].append(t+1)
        if t in x.window:reports.append({'step':t,'before':before,'after':dict(state),'contributions':dict(contributions),'events':events})
    ts=x.declared_T
    eligible=all(state[s]>eps and formation[s] for s in ts)
    sources={origin for site in ts for origin,t in signal_marks[site]}
    trace=tri_and(bool(sources),eligible,x.identity,x.trace_history)
    pw=tri_or(part); hw=tri_or(hdyn)
    return {'trace':trace,'part':pw,'hdyn':hw,'Q':tri_and(trace,pw,hw),'reports':reports,'physical_certificate':False}
~~~

&nbsp;&nbsp;\[通过\] X5c 「三处理等效」在反馈开启时被打破：同一观察末点（第 15 年末）三类处理的末湖位两两不同（改配入湖 0.9404 最高，外运 0.8729，周期移除 0.8734），首次承担时点不全相同  
&nbsp;&nbsp;\[通过\] X5d 两种沙通量判据（出渠、实际入湖）在八臂上的职责登记串逐位相同；物质账不同（改配入湖的入湖盆合计大于外运臂，外运臂外运 \> 0）

L0　冻结申报（计算前写定，正文 8.5）  
&nbsp;&nbsp;候选律 L\*：Rel(L\*) = R\*\_θ(P)，θ = 2/3：事件 i 到来时 P 路承担主要通流（Q\_P ≥ θQ\_i）且主要出渠输沙（S\_P \> 0 且 S\_P ≥ θ(S\_P + S\_Q)）  
&nbsp;&nbsp;适用覆盖域 I = I\_cov = {B, S}；承载组织 R = {u\_P, c\_P}；R 外迹应位 {m\_P, u\_Q, c\_Q, m\_Q}；窗 W = 8 宏步（以 t 为末端），等分 2 段，复走记录判据 ≥ 2  
&nbsp;&nbsp;合法反事实族（DynamicRole）：回退相对静置参照的增量（保留预置基底；双位、单 u\_P、单 c\_P）与设回基态；他位固定  
&nbsp;&nbsp;工作域：𝔐21 缺省参数；h 为供沙边界，于一切反事实与设回中保持实际值不变；V 只记账不回写
